- **efo-importer**: Imports terms from external ontologies (MONDO, UBERON, CL, CHEBI, GO, OBI, HP, PR, OBA, etc.) into EFO via OLS. Searches OLS, verifies the term bidirectionally, adds the IRI to the correct iri_dependencies file, and regenerates the import. Does not edit efo-edit.owl and does not commit.
